# Index — sources/ (ig-atoms-en-batch2)

Instagram English batch 2 (chat ig-atoms-en-batch2): 3,879 assigned codes (ig_artgrowthclub_DRRY-yQjADa → ig_coachjoedrake_CmtP_3Foym8), 728,226 words exactly, 24 accounts (top: bmarkfit 790; avayuergens 477; chase.h.ai 383; coachjoedrake 368). Every assigned code had its .txt in the public repo moaaaz4568-prog/instagram-reels-texts (machine-verified 3879/3879, 0 missing, 0 ambiguous); 186 codes excluded upstream by the user (already have atom files or in-flight elsewhere — not in this list, never worked here). Effective sources: 3847 (728,004 words). Excluded: 32 sources with no quotable content of 40+ chars (whole text under 40 chars, or every span of that length sits across double-quote barriers so no literal quote without nested quotes exists) — listed below with reasons. All atoms/quotes are verbatim in the source language (English except a few accounts' own languages, e.g. aspirenest0b9 Hindi); nothing is translated. Largest source ig_bmarkfit_BvhnmsBHuJD (1,196 words, sliding-window covered). Quotes fold whitespace (ws-fold byte-exact rule).
